{"forms":[{"form":"t-conorms","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The t-conorm #92;sqrt#123;x²#43;y²-x²y²#125; is dominated by the t-conorm x#43;y-xy, which is in turn dominated by the t-conorm #123;x#43;y#92;over 1#43;xy#125;.","type":"example"},{"text":"A t-conorm acts as a disjunction in fuzzy logic or as a union in fuzzy set theory. When one of its arguments is 0, it returns its other argument; when one of its arguments is 1, it returns 1. It is both associative and commutative, and its partial derivatives with respect to its parameters are non-negative.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A binary function from [0,1] × [0,1] to [0,1], which, when given (a,b) as input, returns one minus a t-norm of (1 − a, 1 − b)."],"qualifier":"fuzzy logic"}],"synonyms":[{"word":"s-norm"}],"upstream_line":4288407,"upstream_offset":10422865121,"upstream_record_sha256":"7e91a03616dff87a34b1fb01454108e346ffa47da1e82a9a6cf9452dba5465ff","word":"t-conorm"}
